        "template": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\nWORKER_URL=\"${WORKER_URL:?set WORKER_URL}\"\nKEY=\"${KEY:?set KEY}\"\n\nFROM=\"${FROM:-0}\"\nLIMIT=\"${LIMIT:-1000}\"\nOUT=\"${OUT:-export.ndjson}\"\n\n: > \"$OUT\"\n\nwhile true; do\n  RES=\"$(curl -sS \"$WORKER_URL/export?from=$FROM&limit=$LIMIT\" -H \"x-deathstar-key: $KEY\")\"\n\n  # append blocks to NDJSON\n  python - \"$OUT\" <<'PY' <<<\"$RES\"\nimport json,sys\nout=sys.argv[1]\nj=json.loads(sys.stdin.read())\nwith open(out,'a',encoding='utf-8') as f:\n  for b in j.get('blocks',[]):\n    f.write(json.dumps(b,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':')) + \"\\n\")\nPY\n\n  # read next cursor\n  NEXT=\"$(python - <<'PY' <<<\"$RES\"\nimport json,sys\nj=json.loads(sys.stdin.read())\nn=j.get('next')\nprint(\"\" if n is None else n)\nPY\n  )\"\n\n  if [[ -z \"$NEXT\" ]]; then\n    break\n  fi\n\n  FROM=\"$NEXT\"\ndone\n\npython - <<'PY' \"$OUT\"\nimport hashlib,sys\np=sys.argv[1]\nh=hashlib.sha256(open(p,'rb').read()).hexdigest()\nprint(\"EXPORT_SHA256\",h)\nPY\n"

        "template": "param(\n  [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WORKER_URL,\n  [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$KEY\n)\n\n$Headers = @{ 'x-deathstar-key' = $KEY; 'Content-Type'='application/json' }\n\nWrite-Host \"HEALTH\"\ntry { Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/health\" -Headers $Headers -Method Get | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 } catch { Write-Host $_ }\n\nWrite-Host \"HALT\"\nInvoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/halt\" -Headers $Headers -Method Post -Body '{\"reason\":\"smoke\"}' | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\ntry {\n  Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/emit\" -Headers $Headers -Method Post -Body '{\"actor\":\"A\",\"op\":\"EMIT\",\"nonce\":1,\"commandid\":\"C1\",\"payload\":{\"x\":1}}'\n} catch { Write-Host \"Expected HALTED:\"; Write-Host $_ }\nInvoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/unhalt\" -Headers $Headers -Method Post | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\n\nWrite-Host \"EMIT IDEMPOTENT\"\n$r1 = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/emit\" -Headers $Headers -Method Post -Body '{\"actor\":\"A\",\"op\":\"EMIT\",\"nonce\":1,\"commandid\":\"C1\",\"payload\":{\"x\":1}}'\n$r2 = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/emit\" -Headers $Headers -Method Post -Body '{\"actor\":\"A\",\"op\":\"EMIT\",\"nonce\":2,\"commandid\":\"C1\",\"payload\":{\"x\":999}}'\n$r1 | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\n$r2 | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\n\nWrite-Host \"VERIFY\"\nInvoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/verify\" -Headers $Headers -Method Get | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\n\nWrite-Host \"EXPORT\"\nInvoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$WORKER_URL/export?from=0&limit=1000\" -Headers $Headers -Method Get | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6\n"
      }
    },
    "stage": "5_OF_5",
    "stage5_output_contract": {
      "unknowns": [
        "worker_base_url",
        "master_key_value",
        "r2_enabled",
        "sharded"
      ],
      "what_this_is": "Single payload that consolidates all attached artifacts into a reproducible, testable handoff bundle.",
      "what_to_generate_next": [
        "Run smoke suite against active worker (if any).",
        "Export NDJSON pack and persist checkpoint hash.",
        "If M2: run load test + journal/shard proofs before unlocking."
      ]
    }
  });

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Deterministic canonical JSON (sort keys, stable arrays) for sealing
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function canonicalJSON(x) {
    if (x === null || typeof x !== "object") return JSON.stringify(x);
    if (Array.isArray(x)) return "[" + x.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";
    const keys = Object.keys(x).sort();
    let out = "{";
    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const k = keys[i];
      out += JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(x[k]);
      if (i !== keys.length - 1) out += ",";
    }
    out += "}";
    return out;
  }

  async function sha256HexUtf8(str) {
    if (!globalThis.crypto || !crypto.subtle) return null;
    const data = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  }

  async function verifySeal() {
    const canon = canonicalJSON(PAYLOAD);
    const got = await sha256HexUtf8(canon);
    return Object.freeze({
      ok: got ? (got === SEAL.payload_sha256) : null,
      computed_sha256: got,
      expected_sha256: SEAL.payload_sha256,
      note: got ? "computed via WebCrypto" : "crypto.subtle unavailable; cannot compute in this runtime"
    });
  }

  // Public export (monolithic handoff)
  const PHOENIX_MONOLITH_HANDOFF = Object.freeze({
    seal: SEAL,
    payload: PAYLOAD,
    verifySeal
  });

  globalThis.PHOENIX_MONOLITH_HANDOFF = PHOENIX_MONOLITH_HANDOFF;

  if (typeof console !== "undefined") {
    console.log("PHOENIX_STAGE_5_LOADED ✅", {
      stage: PAYLOAD.stage,
      name: PAYLOAD.name,
      build_time_utc: PAYLOAD.build_time_utc,
      total_files: PAYLOAD.manifest.total_files,
      total_bytes: PAYLOAD.manifest.total_bytes,
      total_lines: PAYLOAD.manifest.total_lines,
      payload_sha256: SEAL.payload_sha256
    });
  }

  return PHOENIX_MONOLITH_HANDOFF;
})();

    "FAIL_if": ["Invented provenance", "Summarized verbatim-required content during ingest"]
  }
},

  "intent": "Audit .codex specs + manifests for drift: missing proofs, contradictions, hidden transitions, role collapse, ledger bypass risks.",
  "default_mode": "SHADOW",
  "inputs_required": ["ROUTING_CANON_V1.json", "LEDGER_EVENT_SCHEMA_V1.json", "DRIFT_GATE_RULES_V1.json", "INGEST_MANIFEST.json|UNKNOWN"],
  "outputs": ["AUDIT_REPORT.json", "DRIFT_REPORT.json", "FIX_LIST.md"],
  "ledger_events_minimum": [
    "AUDIT_FINDING",
    "DRIFT_FLAG",
    "FAIL_CLOSED (if required inputs missing)"
  ],
  "steps": [
    {
      "id": "wf03_s1_validate_inputs",
      "action": "validate_presence",
      "fail_closed_if": ["ROUTING_CANON missing", "LEDGER_EVENT_SCHEMA missing", "DRIFT_GATE_RULES missing"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf03_s2_check_routing_consistency",
      "action": "verify_routing",
      "rules": ["default provider defined", "fallback chains complete", "task_type coverage explicit"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf03_s3_check_ledger_enforcement",
      "action": "verify_ledger_hooks",
      "rules": ["every workflow step declares ledger emission points", "before/after required for effects", "shadow-first enforced"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf03_s4_check_hidden_transitions",
      "action": "scan_for_hidden_transitions",
      "rules": ["no silent mode flips", "state_from/action/state_to required"]

    {
      "id": "wf03_s5_emit_outputs",
      "action": "write_outputs",
      "files": ["AUDIT_REPORT.json", "DRIFT_REPORT.json", "FIX_LIST.md"]
    }
  ],
  "pass_fail": {
    "PASS_if": ["All drift categories have at least one detection rule", "All FAIL_CLOSED conditions are explicit"],
    "FAIL_if": ["Any workflow can mutate state without ticket", "Any claim is made without source_ref or UNKNOWN"]
  }
},

"WF04_PATCH_MINIMAL_CHANGE": {
  "intent": "Apply minimal patches to .codex artifacts with rollback plan; always shadow-test first; produce a patch ledger trail.",
  "default_mode": "SHADOW",
  "inputs_required": ["FIX_LIST.md|UNKNOWN", "target_files[]|UNKNOWN", "capability_ticket|UNKNOWN"],
  "outputs": ["PATCH_PLAN.json", "PATCH_DIFF.txt", "ROLLBACK_PLAN.md"],
  "ledger_events_minimum": [
    "BUILD_PLAN (patch plan)",
    "FILE_WRITE (diff/plan) — SHADOW unless ticket exists",
    "CAPABILITY_TICKET_USED (LIVE only)",
    "FAIL_CLOSED (if LIVE requested without ticket)"
  ],
  "steps": [
    {
      "id": "wf04_s1_validate_inputs",
      "action": "validate_schema",
      "fail_closed_if": ["LIVE requested and no ticket"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf04_s2_shadow_patch",
      "action": "apply_patch_shadow",
      "rules": ["minimal diff", "no scope creep", "attach evidence_refs per change"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf04_s3_shadow_verify",
      "action": "verify_shadow",
      "checks": ["schema valid", "routing still deterministic", "ledger emission points intact", "no hidden transitions"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf04_s4_prepare_rollback",
      "action": "write_rollback_plan",
      "rules": ["single-step revert", "file list exact", "restore prior hashes|UNKNOWN"]
    },
    {
      "id": "wf04_s5_optional_live_apply",
      "action": "apply_live_if_ticket",
      "condition": "ticket present AND explicit authorization in task payload",
      "fail_closed_if": ["ticket scope mismatch"]
    }
  ],
  "pass_fail": {
    "PASS_if": ["Shadow patch verifies clean", "Rollback plan is explicit"],
    "FAIL_if": ["Live writes without ticket", "Patch introduces new undocumented files"]
  }
},

"WF05_PROMOTE_SHADOW_TO_LIVE": {
  "intent": "Formal promotion ceremony: freeze versions, attach proofs, commit ledger events, and seal monolith handoff.",
  "default_mode": "SHADOW (promotion itself is LIVE-gated)",

    "AUDIT_FINDING (final)",

}

"RB01_BOOTSTRAP_CODEX_BRAIN": {

},
"RB03_TOKEN_LIMIT_OR_CONTEXT_LOSS": {

}
},
